Check Findings and Next Steps

  • Preserved original candidate issue: the cited source types do not support the selected assessment scope. A record of a statement does not establish that the described event occurred.

    Calculations and Open Questions

    Calculations are shown separately from explicit rules. Unspecified charging conditions still require clarification. Distance or fare differences alone do not establish liability or a refund.

    • trip_wait480 seconds / 8 minutesE-005 E-006 E-035 E-036 E-037 E-039
    • app_wait_5470 seconds / 7.83333 minutesE-006 E-028 E-035 E-036 E-037 E-039
    • scheduled_pickup_to_cancellation360 seconds / 6 minutesE-003 E-006 E-035 E-036 E-037 E-039
    • Policy Free-Wait Window5 minutes from the scheduled pickup time; ends at: 2026-09-13T08:50:00+08:00P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
    • Advisory Only · WAIT_START_DISAGREEMENT

      Order and app timer start records produce different elapsed times. Preserve both calculations. Section 2.1 explicitly starts the free wait at scheduled pickup; the logged timers remain separate records.

      P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
    • Advisory Only · WAIT_START_BEFORE_SCHEDULED_PICKUP

      Recorded wait starts precede the scheduled pickup; whether early waiting counts is not established by the data. Section 2.1 explicitly starts the free wait at scheduled pickup; the logged timers remain separate records.

      P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
    • Policy or Data Supplement Required · NO_SHOW_THRESHOLD_CLOCK_UNSPECIFIED

      The embedded no-show threshold has no defined start event. Section 2.1 defines free wait from scheduled pickup but does not define this separate threshold clock.

      P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
    • Policy or Data Supplement Required · POLICY_SOURCE_CONFLICT

      Embedded cancellation thresholds/fee differ from standalone v2.1 sections 2.1-2.2 (cancel after 5 free minutes from scheduled pickup; $5 fee). Source precedence is not supplied. Do not treat 5+8 as 13 or choose the favorable clock.

      P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.2
    • Advisory Only · FRAUD_FLAG_CONFIDENCE_REQUIREMENT

      Section 6.3 raises the required confidence to 95%. This is not proof of present fault and no confidence value is fabricated.

      P-RYDE-2.1-S6.3

    How the Judge Assesses Both Sides

    The analysis below passed structural and reference checks. Those checks do not establish that the natural-language reasoning is correct.

    Core Question

    Whether the $5.00 no-show cancellation fee charged to the rider was contractually valid given the conflict between embedded 8-minute threshold rule P-004 and Ryde Policy v2.1 Section 2.1 5-minute wait rule, and the rider profile fraud flag requiring 95% confidence.

    Rider Position · Partly Supported

    The rider correctly highlights the unresolved policy conflict between P-004 and Section 2.1, the premature cancellation if 8 minutes run from scheduled pickup, and the elevated 95% confidence requirement, though the rider's personal statement of presence is unverified.

    Arguments: opening-rider-claims-0 opening-rider-claims-1 opening-rider-claims-2 opening-rider-claims-3 opening-rider-counterpoints-0 opening-rider-counterpoints-1 response-rider-claims-0 response-rider-claims-1 response-rider-claims-2 response-rider-claims-3 response-rider-counterpoints-0 response-rider-counterpoints-1

    Evidence: E-002 E-003 E-006 E-031 E-033 E-037 E-039 P-004 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S6.3

    Driver Position · Partly Supported

    The driver establishes arrival, stationary wait, and compliance with Section 2.3 contact requirements and Section 2.1 free wait from scheduled pickup, but cannot unilaterally resolve the source conflict with embedded policy P-004.

    Arguments: opening-driver-claims-0 opening-driver-claims-1 opening-driver-claims-2 opening-driver-claims-3 opening-driver-counterpoints-0 opening-driver-counterpoints-1 opening-driver-counterpoints-2 response-driver-claims-0 response-driver-claims-1 response-driver-claims-2 response-driver-claims-3 response-driver-counterpoints-0 response-driver-counterpoints-1

    Evidence: E-001 E-003 E-004 E-005 E-006 E-013 E-017 E-018 E-019 E-020 E-021 E-026 E-029 E-031 E-035 E-037 E-039 P-001 P-004 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.2 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.3 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.4

    Material Decision Conditions

    • no_show_charge · Unknown

      Cannot determine validity of the $5.00 fee due to unresolved conflict between embedded 8-minute rule P-004 and Policy v2.1 Section 2.1.

      E-001 E-003 E-004 E-005 E-006 E-007 E-008 E-017 E-018 E-019 E-020 E-021 E-022 E-023 E-024 E-025 E-026 E-027 E-028 E-029 E-030 E-031 E-032 E-035 E-036 E-037 E-038 E-039 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.2 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.3 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.4
    • free_wait · Met

      Under Section 2.1, 5 free minutes ran from scheduled pickup (08:45:00) to 08:50:00, and cancellation occurred at 08:51:00 (360 seconds elapsed).

      E-003 E-006 E-035 E-036 E-037 E-039 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
    • driver_contact · Met

      The driver sent 4 in-app chat messages and initiated an in-app call ringing 22 seconds prior to cancelling, fulfilling Section 2.3.

      E-017 E-018 E-019 E-020 E-021 E-029 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.3
    • driver_arrival · Met

      Recorded arrival event at 08:43:00 within 10m of pickup with speed 0 km/h confirms timely driver arrival.

      E-004 E-026 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.4

    Disputed Issues

    • Applicability and reference start clock of the 8-minute no-show threshold versus the 5-minute free wait rule

      Under v2.1 Section 2.1, the driver waited 6 minutes past scheduled pickup, satisfying the 5-minute rule. However, embedded rule P-004 specifies an 8-minute threshold whose start anchor (arrival at 08:43:00 vs scheduled pickup at 08:45:00) is undefined and in conflict with v2.1.

      E-003 E-004 E-006 E-031 E-035 E-037 E-039 P-004 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.2
    • Elevation of confidence threshold due to rider fraud flag

      Under Section 6.3, because the rider profile has 1 fraud flag, the required confidence threshold is raised to 95%, which cannot be satisfied autonomously while core policy rules remain contradictory.

      E-033 P-RYDE-2.1-S6.3

    Review Issues and Dispositions

    • NO_SHOW_THRESHOLD_CLOCK_UNSPECIFIED · Clarification Still Required

      Embedded policy P-004 does not define whether the 8-minute threshold counts from driver arrival, wait timer initiation, or scheduled pickup time, leaving the clock undefined.

      E-003 E-004 E-005 E-028 E-031 P-004 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
    • POLICY_SOURCE_CONFLICT · Clarification Still Required

      There is an unresolved contradiction between embedded cancellation policy P-004 (8-minute threshold) and standalone Policy v2.1 Section 2.1 (cancellation allowed 5 minutes after scheduled pickup). Source hierarchy is missing.

      E-003 E-006 E-031 E-037 E-039 P-004 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.2

    Missing Information

    • Official determination of precedence between embedded policy P-004 and Ryde Policy v2.1 Section 2.1.
    • Authoritative definition of the anchor start event for the 8-minute no-show threshold clock in rule P-004.

    Precedent Comparison

    • P-RYDE-2.1-S7.3

      Similarities: Both involve a no-show cancellation fee of $5.00 after the driver arrived at pickup and waited past the free wait window.

      Differences: In precedent DISP-2026-0211, the driver sent zero messages and made zero calls, violating Section 2.3, whereas here the driver sent 4 messages and made 1 call, fully satisfying Section 2.3.

    Bound Policy and Program Checks

    RYDE-POLICY-V2.1 · Effective 2026-01-01

    Human-Review Threshold

    • NO_SHOW_THRESHOLD_CLOCK_UNSPECIFIED The embedded no-show threshold has no defined start event. Section 2.1 defines free wait from scheduled pickup but does not define this separate threshold clock. P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
    • POLICY_SOURCE_CONFLICT Embedded cancellation thresholds/fee differ from standalone v2.1 sections 2.1-2.2 (cancel after 5 free minutes from scheduled pickup; $5 fee). Source precedence is not supplied. Do not treat 5+8 as 13 or choose the favorable clock. P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.2

    Policy Conditions

    • no_show_charge · unknown

      Fixed fee requires reconciled policy, sufficient scheduled free wait, arrival and both contact attempts.

      E-001 E-003 E-004 E-005 E-006 E-007 E-008 E-017 E-018 E-019 E-020 E-021 E-022 E-023 E-024 E-025 E-026 E-027 E-028 E-029 E-030 E-031 E-032 E-035 E-036 E-037 E-038 E-039 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.2 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.3 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.4
    • free_wait · met

      Five free minutes start at scheduled pickup; exact-boundary cancellation requires clarification.

      E-003 E-006 E-035 E-036 E-037 E-039 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
    • driver_contact · met

      At least one in-app message AND one call attempt strictly before cancellation. Missing logs alone do not prove no attempt; conflicting complete summaries require review.

      E-017 E-018 E-019 E-020 E-021 E-029 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.3
    • driver_arrival · met

      Recorded arrival event corroborates timely arrival; driver messages alone are not location evidence.

      E-004 E-026 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.4

    Conservative Program Adjustments

    The program preserves the original output, downgrades argument ratings that lack the required source support or reconciles summary labels with individual assessments, then rechecks all rules. For Fare, these adjustments are allowed only when the material policy conditions and main supporting evidence independently hold. The recommendation and amount remain unchanged; no charge or refund is executed.

    Rider Advocate

    Opening Argument

    • The rider reported waiting at the lobby pickup point at Tiong Bahru Plaza on time for 10 minutes and being unable to find the vehicle, disputing the fairness of the $5.00 no-show cancellation fee. E-002 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.4
    • The audit identifies an unresolvable source policy conflict and an unspecified clock between embedded cancellation policy P-004 (requiring an 8-minute no-show threshold) and Ryde Policy v2.1 Section 2.1 (permitting cancellation after a 5-minute free wait from scheduled pickup), precluding a definitive entitlement to charge. E-003 E-006 E-031 E-037 E-039 P-004 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.2
    • Under Ryde Policy v2.1 Section 2.1, the 5-minute free wait starts strictly at the scheduled pickup time of 08:45:00, meaning only 360 seconds (6 minutes) elapsed prior to the 08:51:00 cancellation; if embedded rule P-004's 8-minute threshold applies from scheduled time, the cancellation occurred prematurely. E-003 E-006 E-037 E-039 P-004 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
    • Because the rider profile has 1 fraud flag, Section 6.3 elevates the agent confidence requirement to 95%, which cannot be attained in the presence of unresolved policy threshold conflicts. E-033 P-RYDE-2.1-S6.3
    • App and communication logs show the driver sent four chat messages, placed an unanswered call ringing for 22 seconds, and recorded GPS arrival at 08:43:00, which satisfies the procedural contact obligations of Section 2.3. E-001 E-013 E-017 E-018 E-019 E-020 E-021 E-026 E-029 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.3
    • While discrete GPS coordinates show the driver stopped at the plaza coordinates, periodic GPS samples alone do not prove continuous visibility or uninterrupted presence at the specific lobby entrance where the rider reported waiting. E-001 E-002 E-013 E-014 E-015 E-016
    • Clarification on the legal and contractual hierarchy between the embedded 8-minute no-show threshold (P-004) and Ryde Policy v2.1 Section 2.1 (5-minute wait from scheduled pickup).
    • Specification of the triggering start event for the embedded 8-minute no-show threshold (whether it begins at driver arrival, wait timer start, or scheduled pickup).
    • Independent verification of driver visibility or precise positioning at the specific lobby pickup bay where the rider reported waiting.

    Response to the Other Side

    • The rider reported arriving at the designated Tiong Bahru Plaza pickup lobby on time and waiting 10 minutes without locating the driver, directly disputing the fairness of the $5.00 no-show cancellation fee. E-002 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.4
    • A material policy conflict exists between embedded policy P-004 (mandating an 8-minute no-show threshold) and Ryde Policy v2.1 Section 2.1 (permitting cancellation 5 minutes after scheduled pickup); if the 8-minute threshold governs from scheduled pickup (08:45:00), the cancellation at 08:51:00 (360 seconds elapsed) was premature. E-003 E-006 E-037 E-039 P-004 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
    • Although the driver arrived early at 08:43:00, Policy v2.1 Section 2.1 establishes that the free wait period begins strictly at the scheduled pickup time of 08:45:00, meaning early arrival cannot compress rider waiting rights. E-003 E-004 E-026 E-028 E-039 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
    • Because an unresolvable source conflict and unspecified reference clock persist regarding the 8-minute threshold, and Section 6.3 mandates 95% confidence due to the profile fraud flag without providing a basis for resolution, the dispute cannot be summarily decided by an automated ruling. E-031 E-033 P-RYDE-2.1-S6.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S6.3
    • Opponent establishes that the driver fulfilled pre-cancellation contact rules under Section 2.3 by sending four in-app messages and placing one in-app call ringing 22 seconds before cancelling. E-017 E-018 E-019 E-020 E-021 E-029 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.3
    • Opponent shows GPS telemetry and system logs recorded the driver stationary at pickup coordinates from 08:43:00 to 08:51:00, though telemetry samples alone do not prove continuous physical visibility to the rider at the lobby entrance. E-001 E-013 E-014 E-015 E-016 E-026 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1
    • Resolution of precedence between embedded policy P-004 (8-minute no-show threshold) and Ryde Policy v2.1 Section 2.1 (5-minute free wait from scheduled pickup).
    • Explicit definition of the anchor clock for the 8-minute no-show threshold under P-004 (driver arrival at 08:43:00 vs scheduled pickup at 08:45:00).

    Driver Advocate

    Opening Argument

    • Under Policy v2.1 Section 2.1, the 5-minute free wait began at scheduled pickup (08:45:00) and expired at 08:50:00, meaning the driver waited 6 full minutes (360 seconds) past scheduled pickup before cancelling at 08:51:00. E-003 E-006 E-037 E-039 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S2.2

    Response to the Other Side

    • The system wait timer and cancellation logs show a recorded wait duration of 480 seconds (8 minutes) from arrival at 08:43:00 to cancellation at 08:51:00, which triggered the embedded 8-minute no-show threshold and applied the $5.00 fee. E-005 E-006 E-008 E-022 E-028 E-030 E-031 E-035 P-001 P-002 P-004
